Appendix A

Auxiliary results

Lemma A.1

(Lemma 3 in
(
Jamieson et al. 2014
)
)
.

Let
\{X_{i}\}_{i=1}^{\infty}
be a sequence of i.i.d. centered sub-Gaussian random variables with scale parameter
\sigma
. Fix any
\epsilon\in(0,1)
and
\delta\in(0,\ln(1+\epsilon)/e)
. Then one has

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[\forall\,t\!\in\!\mathbb{N}:\!\sum_{s=1}^{t}X_{s}\!\leq\!(1\!+\!\sqrt{\epsilon})\sqrt{2\sigma^{2}\left(1\!+\!\epsilon\right)t\ln\left(\frac{\ln\left((1\!+\!\epsilon)t\right)}{\delta}\right)}\bigg]\geq 1-\xi(\delta),

(42)

where
\xi(\delta):=\frac{2+\epsilon}{\epsilon}\big(\frac{\delta}{\ln(1+\epsilon)}\big)^{1+\epsilon}
.

Lemma A.2
.

For
t\geq 1,\epsilon\in(0,1),\omega\in(0,1]
and
u>0
, let

\gamma:=\frac{(1+\epsilon)(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}}{2}
,

c:=\frac{(u\cdot s)^{2}}{\gamma}=\frac{2(u\cdot s)^{2}}{(1+\epsilon)(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}}
and

m:=\frac{\gamma}{u^{2}\cdot s^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{s^{2}}+\ln\frac{2\gamma(1+\epsilon)}{u^{2}}\right)
.
If
t>m
, it holds that

s>\mathrm{lil}(t,\omega)=(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\sqrt{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2t}\ln\left(\frac{\ln\left((1\!+\!\epsilon)t\right)}{\omega}\right)}.

Proof of Lemma
A.2
.

Note that fact that

\displaystyle u\cdot s\leq(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\sqrt{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2t}\ln\left(\frac{\ln\left((1\!+\!\epsilon)t\right)}{\omega}\right)}\Longleftrightarrow c=\frac{2(u\cdot s)^{2}}{(1+\epsilon)(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{t}\ln\left(\frac{\ln\left((1\!+\!\epsilon)t\right)}{\omega}\right)

(43)

According to the computations in
Jamieson et al. 2014
equation (1), i.e.,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t}\ln\left(\frac{\ln((1+\epsilon)t)}{\omega}\right)\geq c^{\prime}\Rightarrow t\leq\frac{1}{c^{\prime}}\ln\left(\frac{2\ln((1+\epsilon)/(c^{\prime}\omega))}{\omega}\right)

(44)

for
t\geq 1,\epsilon\in(0,1),c^{\prime}>0,\omega\in(0,1]
.
We take
c^{\prime}=c
, thus

\displaystyle t

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{c}\ln\left(\frac{2\ln((1+\epsilon)/(c\omega))}{\omega}\right)

(45)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{c}\left(\ln\frac{2}{\omega}+\ln\left(\ln\frac{\gamma(1+\epsilon)}{u^{2}\cdot\omega}+\ln\frac{1}{s^{2}}\right)\right)

(46)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{1}{c}\left(\ln\frac{2}{\omega}+\ln\frac{\gamma(1+\epsilon)}{u^{2}\cdot\omega}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{s^{2}}\right)

(47)

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma}{u^{2}\cdot s^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{s^{2}}+\ln\frac{2\gamma(1+\epsilon)}{u^{2}}\right)=m

(48)

where we adopt
\ln(x+y)\leq x+\ln\ln_{+}y,\forall x,y\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
in
(a)
.
Therefore, if
t>m
, we must have

u\cdot s>(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\sqrt{\frac{1+\epsilon}{2t}\ln\left(\frac{\ln\left((1\!+\!\epsilon)t\right)}{\omega}\right)}.

∎

Lemma A.3
.

With the choice of the confidence radii in (
6
) and (
7
), for all
i\in E
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\forall\,p\!\in\!\mathbb{N}:|\hat{\mu}_{i}(p)-\mu_{i}|\leq\alpha(T_{i}(p))\right]\geq 1-2\xi(\omega_{\mu})

(49)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\forall\,p\!\in\!\mathbb{N}:|\hat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}(p)-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\leq\beta_{\mathrm{u}}(T_{i}(p))\right]\geq 1-4\xi(\omega_{\mathrm{v}})

(50)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\forall\,p\!\in\!\mathbb{N}:|\hat{\sigma}_{i}^{2}(p)-\sigma_{i}^{2}|\leq\beta_{\mathrm{l}}(T_{i}(p))\right]\geq 1-4\xi(\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})

(51)

Proof.

Note the fact that any distribution supported on
[0,1]
is
1/4
-sub-Gaussian.
By a direct application of Lemma
A.1
to the sample mean
\hat{\mu}_{i}(p)
and the sample second moment
\hat{M}_{2,i}(p):=\frac{1}{T_{i}(p)}\sum_{s=1}^{p}W_{i}(s)^{2}\mathbbm{1}\{i\in A_{s}\}
of arm
i\in[L]
, (
49
) can be derived and

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\forall\,p\!\in\!\mathbb{N}:|\mu_{i}-\hat{\mu}_{i}(p)|\leq\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p),\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})\right]\geq 1-2\xi(\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}),\quad\mbox{and}

(53)

\displaystyle\;\mathbb{P}\left[\forall\,p\!\in\!\mathbb{N}:|\hat{M}_{2,i}(p)-(\mu_{i}^{2}+\sigma_{i}^{2})|\leq\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p),\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})\right]\geq 1-2\xi(\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})

(54)

Since the rewards are in
[0,1]
,
|\mu_{i}^{2}-\hat{\mu}_{i}^{2}(p)|=|\mu_{i}+\hat{\mu}_{i}(p)|\cdot|\mu_{i}-\hat{\mu}_{i}(p)|\leq 2\cdot\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p),\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})
. Using this and the
triangle inequality, we obtain for every
p\geq 1
,

\displaystyle|\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{i}(p)-\sigma_{i}^{2}|

\displaystyle=|\mu_{i}^{2}-\hat{\mu}_{i}^{2}(p)|+|(\mu_{i}^{2}+\sigma_{i}^{2})-\hat{M}_{2,i}(p)|

(55)

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p),\omega_{\mathrm{v}})+\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p),\omega_{\mathrm{v}})=\beta_{\mathrm{u}}(T_{i}(p)).

(56)

Therefore, (
50
) is proved. (
51
) can be similarly obtained.
∎

Appendix B

Proof of the Upper Bound

B.1

Proof scheme of the problem-dependent upper bound

In this subsection, we provide technical lemmas that can upper bound the components in
\mathrm{R}_{1}(T^{\prime})
and
\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})
.

Note that at phase
p
, the identified solution
A_{p}
belongs to one of the
4
disjoint sets: (1)
A_{p}=S^{\star}
; (2)
\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
; (3)
\mathcal{R}
and (4)
\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}
, i.e.

\displaystyle 1

\displaystyle=\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}=S^{\star}\right\}+\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}+\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\right\}+\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}

(57)

and
\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{B}\right\}=\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}+\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}
.
Define two events (the
\mathcal{F}
events) that connect the instance and the confidence radii

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{\Delta_{A_{p}}\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}\alpha(T_{i}(p-1))\bigg\}

(58)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\rho)

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{x\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\rho)\bigg\}

(59)

where
x
is a constant and
\omega
is a confidence parameter.
When
A_{p}\in\mathcal{B}
, it indicates solution
A_{p}
has not been sampled sufficiently many times and its suboptimality has not been ascertained.
When
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}
, it implies the unsafeness of
A_{p}
has not been recognized.
We formalize this in the following lemma.

Lemma B.1
.

Conditional on the event
\mathcal{E}
,
given any
p\in[T]
, we have

•

S^{\star}\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{p-1}
;

•

If
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}\right\};

(60)

•

If
A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}
,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\};

(61)

•

If
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}
,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu},\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}.

(62)

Proof of Lemma
B.1
.

By the design of
PASCombUCB
,
A_{p}\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{p-1}
.

(1) We firstly prove that
S\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{p-1},\forall S\in\mathcal{S}
. On the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle L_{S}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in A}\max\{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{i}(p-1)-\beta_{\mathrm{l}}(T_{i}(p-1)),0\}

(63)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in A}\max\{\sigma^{2}_{i},0\}

(64)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{S}^{2}<\bar{\sigma}^{2}

(65)

Thus,
S\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{p-1}
, and in particular,
S^{\star}\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{p-1}
.

(2) If
A_{p}\in\mathcal{B}
, according to the sampling strategy in Line
10
of
PASCombUCB
and
S^{\star}\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{p-1}
,
we have
U_{S^{\star}}^{\mu}(p-1)\leq U_{A_{p}}^{\mu}(p-1)

which indicates

\sum_{i\in S^{\star}\setminus A_{p}}U_{i}^{\mu}(p-1)\leq\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}U_{i}^{\mu}(p-1)
.
Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in S^{\star}\setminus A_{p}}\mu_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in S^{\star}\setminus A_{p}}U_{i}^{\mu}(p-1)

(66)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}U_{i}^{\mu}(p-1)

(67)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}\mu_{i}+2\alpha_{i}(T_{i}(p-1))

(68)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\qquad

\displaystyle\Delta_{A_{p}}\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}\alpha(T_{i}(p-1))

(69)

(3) If
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}
, according to the sampling strategy,
we have
A_{p}\in\bar{\mathcal{S}}_{p-1}

which indicates

L_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)=\sum_{i\in A_{p}}L_{i}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)<\bar{\sigma}^{2}
.
Thus,

\displaystyle\bar{\sigma}^{2}

\displaystyle>\hat{\sigma}_{A_{p}}^{2}(p-1)-\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\beta_{\mathrm{l}}(T_{i}(p-1))

(70)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{A_{p}}^{2}-2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\beta_{\mathrm{l}}(T_{i}(p-1))

(71)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\qquad

\displaystyle\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}\beta_{\mathrm{l}}(T_{i}(p-1))

(72)

Note that if
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
, according to (
69
),

\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}\right\}.

If
A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\subset\mathcal{S}^{c}
, by (
72
)

\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}.

If
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}
, by (
72
) and (
69
)

\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu},\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}

∎

At phase
p
, we define two sequences of mutually-exclusive events
\{\mathcal{G}^{\mu}_{j,p}\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}}
and
\{\mathcal{G}_{j,p}(x,\omega\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}}
(the
\mathcal{G}
events) which can further bound the number of times
\mathcal{F}^{\mu}_{p}
and
\mathcal{F}^{\mathrm{v}}_{p}(x,\omega)
occur respectively.
These events are indexed by two strictly-decreasing sequences of constants:

\displaystyle a_{1}

\displaystyle>a_{2}>\ldots>a_{k}>\ldots

(73)

\displaystyle 1

\displaystyle>b_{1}>b_{2}>\ldots>b_{k}>\ldots

(74)

where
\lim_{j\to\infty}a_{j}=\lim_{j\to\infty}b_{j}=0
. For simplicity, we set
a_{j}=\frac{4}{9^{j-2}}
,
b_{j}=\frac{1}{4^{j}},\forall j\in\mathbb{N}
and denote the constant
C=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}}{b_{j}}=259.2
. For
x\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
and
\omega\in(0,\ln(1+\epsilon)/e)
, define

\displaystyle m_{j}(x,\omega):=\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{x^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{x^{2}}+D\right)

(75)

and
m_{j}(x,\omega):=\infty
otherwise,
where
(1)
\gamma=\frac{(1+\epsilon)(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}}{2}
and
\epsilon
is the constant in the confidence bounds (
7
),
(2)
\ln\ln_{+}(x)=\ln\ln x
if
x\geq e
and it equals to
0
otherwise,
(3)
D=\ln\left(324K^{2}(1+\epsilon)^{2}(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}\right)
.
Denote

\displaystyle G_{j,p}^{\mu}

\displaystyle:=\left\{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}:T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu})\right\}\quad\text{and}

(76)

\displaystyle G_{j,p}(x,\omega)

\displaystyle:=\left\{i\in A_{p}:T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}(x,\omega)\right\}

(77)

as the sets of items that were not chosen sufficiently often.
For
j\in\mathbb{N}
, the events at phase
p
are sequentially defined as

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{\mu}_{j,p}

\displaystyle:=\Big\{\text{at least }b_{j}K\text{ items in }A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}\text{ were chosen}

(78)

\displaystyle\text{ at most }m_{j}(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu})\text{ times}\Big\}\bigcap\left(\bigcup_{k\in[j-1]}\mathcal{G}^{\mu}_{k,p}\right)^{c}

(79)

\displaystyle=\Big\{\left|G_{j,p}^{\mu}\right|\geq b_{j}K\Big\}\bigcap\left(\bigcup_{k\in[j-1]}\mathcal{G}^{\mu}_{k,p}\right)^{c}\qquad\mbox{and}

(80)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{j,p}(x,\omega)

\displaystyle:=\Big\{\text{at least }b_{j}K\text{ items in }A_{p}\text{ were chosen}

(81)

\displaystyle\text{ at most }m_{j}(x,\omega)\text{ times}\Big\}\bigcap\left(\bigcup_{k\in[j-1]}\mathcal{G}_{k,p}(x,\omega)\right)^{c}

(82)

\displaystyle=\Big\{\left|G_{j,p}(x,\omega)\right|\geq b_{j}K\Big\}\bigcap\left(\bigcup_{k\in[j-1]}\mathcal{G}_{k,p}(x,\omega)\right)^{c}

(83)

Lemma B.2
.

With our choice of
\{a_{j}\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}}
and
\{b_{j}\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}}
,

•

if
\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}
occurs,
\mathcal{G}^{\mu}_{j,p}
occurs for some
j
, i.e.

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\bigcup_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathcal{G}^{\mu}_{j,p}\right\}.

(84)

•

if
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)
occurs,
\mathcal{G}_{j,p}(x,\omega)
occurs for some
j
, i.e.

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\bigcup_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathcal{G}_{j,p}(x,\omega)\right\}.

(85)

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.

We prove (
85
) in the following and the other statement can be proved by the same procedures.

To ease the notations, we omit the parameters
x,\omega
and
p
in
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega),\mathcal{G}_{j,p}(x,\omega)
and
m_{j}(x,\omega)
, since they are fixed when given
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)
.
The event
\mathcal{G}_{j}
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{j}

\displaystyle=\Big\{\left|G_{j}\right|\geq b_{j}K\Big\}\bigcap\left(\bigcap_{k\in[j-1]}\mathcal{G}_{k}^{c}\right)

(86)

\displaystyle=\Big\{\left|G_{j}\right|\geq b_{j}K\Big\}\bigcap\left(\bigcap_{k\in[j-1]}\Big\{\left|G_{k}\right|<b_{k}K\Big\}\right)

(87)

The statement is proved by contradiction. We assume that when
\mathcal{F}
(
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)
) occurs, none of event
\mathcal{G}_{j}
occurs.
Hence,

\displaystyle\left(\bigcup_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathcal{G}_{j}\right)^{c}=\bigcap_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathcal{G}_{j}^{c}

(88)

\displaystyle=\bigcap_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\left[\Big\{\left|G_{j}\right|<b_{j}K\Big\}\bigcup\left(\bigcup_{k\in[j-1]}\Big\{\left|G_{k}\right|\geq b_{k}K\Big\}\right)\right]

(89)

\displaystyle=\bigcap_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\Big\{\left|G_{j}\right|<b_{j}K\Big\}

(90)

Let
\bar{G}_{j}:=A_{p}\setminus G_{j}
and define
G_{0}=A_{p}
. According to the definition of
G_{j}
, we have
G_{j}\subset G_{j-1}
and

\bar{G}_{j-1}\subset\bar{G}_{j},\forall j\in\mathbb{N}
. Because
\lim_{j\to\infty}m_{j}=0
, there exists
j_{0}
such that
\bar{G}_{j}=A_{p},\forall j\geq j_{0}
. Therefore, we can write
A_{p}
by the “telescoping” sum, i.e.,
A_{p}=\cup_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\left(\bar{G}_{j}\setminus\bar{G}_{j-1}\right)
.

\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)
indicates

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega)

(91)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\sum_{i\in\bar{G}_{j}\setminus\bar{G}_{j-1}}\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega)

(92)

Note that for
i\in\bar{G}_{j}\setminus\bar{G}_{j-1}=G_{j-1}\setminus G_{j}
, we have
T_{i}(p-1)\in(m_{j},m_{j-1}]
.
By Lemma
A.2
with parameters
t=T_{i}(p-1),s=x,u=\sqrt{\frac{1}{a_{j}K^{2}}}
and note
a_{1}>a_{j}
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{a_{j}K^{2}}}\cdot x>\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega).

(93)

Note our choice of
a_{j}
and
b_{j}
satisfy

\displaystyle 2\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{b_{j-1}-b_{j}}{\sqrt{a_{j}}}\leq 1

(94)

Thus, (
91
) can be further bounded by

\displaystyle x

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\sum_{i\in\bar{G}_{j}\setminus\bar{G}_{j-1}}\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega)

(95)

\displaystyle<2\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}|\bar{G}_{j}\setminus\bar{G}_{j-1}|\sqrt{\frac{1}{a_{j}K^{2}}}\cdot x

(96)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{(b_{j-1}-b_{j})K}{\sqrt{a_{j}}K}x

(97)

\displaystyle\leq x

(98)

which constitutes a contradiction. Thus when
\mathcal{F}
occurs, there must exists
j\in\mathbb{N}
such that
\mathcal{G}_{j}
occurs.
∎

When none of the
\mathcal{G}_{j,p}^{\mu}
(resp.
\mathcal{G}_{j,p}(x,\omega)
) occurs,
\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}
(resp.
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)
) must not occur, which indicates all of the items in
A_{p}
have been sampled sufficiently many times such that the suboptimality (resp. unsafeness) of
A_{p}
is identified, thus
A_{p}
will not been sampled in future phases.

As there will be multiple
\mathcal{F}
events happening, we provide the following useful lemma that merges all
\mathcal{F}
events.

Lemma B.3
.

Given two confidence parameters
\omega_{1}\geq\omega_{2}\in(0,\ln(1+\epsilon)/e)

•

If
\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}
occurs, then
\mathcal{F}_{p}(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu})
occurs.

•

If both events
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega_{1})
and
\mathcal{F}_{p}(y,\omega_{2})
occur, then event

\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\max\{x,\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{1}}}{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{2}}}}y\},\omega_{1}\right)
occurs.

Proof of Lemma
B.3
.

If
\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}
occurs, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{A_{p}}\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}\alpha(T_{i}(p-1))\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\alpha(T_{i}(p-1))=2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega_{\mu})

(99)

Thus,
\mathcal{F}_{p}(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu})
occurs.

For the second statement, notice the fact that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega_{1})}{\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega_{2})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left(1+\sqrt{\epsilon}\right)\Big(\frac{1+\epsilon}{2T_{i}(p-1)}\ln\big(\frac{\ln((1+\epsilon)T_{i}(p-1))}{\omega_{1}}\big)\Big)^{1/2}}{\left(1+\sqrt{\epsilon}\right)\Big(\frac{1+\epsilon}{2t}\ln\big(\frac{\ln((1+\epsilon)T_{i}(p-1))}{\omega_{2}}\big)\Big)^{1/2}}

(100)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{1}}+\ln\ln((1+\epsilon)T_{i}(p-1))}{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{2}}+\ln\ln((1+\epsilon)T_{i}(p-1))}}

(101)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\geq}\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{1}}}{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{2}}}}=:\rho

(102)

where
(a)
utilizes the trick that
\frac{a+c}{b+c}\geq\frac{a}{b},\forall a,b,c\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
and
a\leq b
.
When event
\mathcal{F}_{p}(y,\omega_{2})
occurs,

\displaystyle y\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega_{2})\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\frac{1}{\omega}\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega_{1})

(103)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\quad

\displaystyle\rho\cdot y\leq 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega_{1})

(104)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\quad

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{p}(\rho y,\omega_{1})

(105)

Thus if both events
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega_{1})
and
\mathcal{F}_{p}(y,\omega_{2})
occur, we must have that event

\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\max\{x,\rho y\},\omega_{1})\right)=\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\max\{x,\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{1}}}{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{2}}}}y\},\omega_{1})\right)
occurs.

∎

B.2

Upper bound decomposition

See
6.1

Proof of Lemma
6.1
.

The expected regret can be decomposed as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathrm{R}(T^{\prime})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}(\mu^{\star}-\mu_{A_{p,r}})\right]

(106)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}(\mu^{\star}-\mu_{A_{p,r}})\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{E}\right\}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}(\mu^{\star}-\mu_{A_{p,r}})\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{c}\right\}\right]

(107)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}(\mu^{\star}-\mu_{A_{p,r}})\bigg|\mathcal{E}\right]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}]+\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}(\mu^{\star}-\mu_{A_{p,r}})\bigg|\mathcal{E}^{c}\right]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}^{c}]

(108)

In the initialization stage, it will take at most
2L
time steps, since each pulled solution
A_{p}
contains at least one item
i
with
T_{i}(p)<2
. Thus the regret is at most
2L\cdot\mu^{\star}
.

The expected regret when the good events fail can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}(\mu^{\star}-\mu_{A_{p,r}})\bigg|\mathcal{E}^{c}\right]\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{E}^{c}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}\mu^{\star}\bigg|\mathcal{E}^{c}\right]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}^{c}]

(109)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mu^{\star}\bigg|\mathcal{E}^{c}\right]L\cdot 2(\xi(\omega_{\mu})+2\xi(\omega_{v})+2\xi(\omega_{v}^{\prime}))

(110)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu^{\star}TL\cdot(\xi(\omega_{\mu})+2\xi(\omega_{v})+2\xi(\omega_{v}^{\prime}))

(111)

where
\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}^{c}]
can be bounded using Lemma
A.3
.

Conditional on the good event
\mathcal{E}
, the high-probability regret can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\hat{\mathrm{R}}(T^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}(\mu^{\star}-\mu_{A_{p,r}})

(112)

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\left[\Delta_{A_{p}}+\mu^{\star}(n_{p}-1)\right]

(113)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\left[\Delta_{A_{p}}+\mu^{\star}\sum_{r=0}^{Q-1}\left(2\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\}-2\right)\right]

(114)

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\left[\Delta_{A_{p}}+\mu^{\star}\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}2\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\}\right]

(115)

where
(a)
makes use of Lemma
B.4
and the fact if

U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)\in((m-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2},m\bar{\sigma}^{2}]
, then
m=\sum_{r=0}^{Q-1}\mathbbm{1}\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\}=\sum_{r=0}^{Q-1}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}
. Note the fact that only the suboptimal solutions yields positive mean gap and that the negative mean gap of the risky solutions can be upper bounded by
0
, thus the above equation can be further divided into two parts

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\left[\Delta_{A_{p}}+\mu^{\star}\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}2\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\}\right]

(116)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{B}\}\Delta_{A_{p}}+\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mu^{\star}\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}2\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\}

(117)

\displaystyle=:\mathrm{R}_{1}(T^{\prime})+\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})

(118)

In conclusion, by summarizing the regret from the initialization stage, the regret due to failure of the good event and the high-probability regret, the expected regret can be bounded by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{R}(T^{\prime})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{R}_{1}(T^{\prime})|\mathcal{E}]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}]+\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})|\mathcal{E}]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{E}]+2\mu^{\star}\cdot TL\left(\xi(\omega_{\mu})+2\xi(\omega_{v})+2\xi(\omega_{v}^{\prime})\right)+2\mu^{\star}L

(119)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{R}_{1}(T^{\prime})|\mathcal{E}]+\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})|\mathcal{E}]+2\mu^{\star}\cdot TL\left(\xi(\omega_{\mu})+2\xi(\omega_{v})+2\xi(\omega_{v}^{\prime})\right)+2\mu^{\star}L

(120)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{R}_{1}(T^{\prime})|\mathcal{E}]+\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})|\mathcal{E}]+\mathrm{R}_{3}(T)

(121)

∎

B.3

Regret due to suboptimality

See
6.2

Proof of Lemma
6.2
.

Notice the fact that the suboptimal solution
A_{p}
can be safe, i.e.
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
, or unsafe, i.e.,
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}
, we upper bound the regret under these the two scenarios separately.

Case
1
:
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}

By the definition of the event
\mathcal{G}_{j,p}^{\mu}
, we have

|G_{j,p}^{\mu}|=\left|\left\{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}:T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu})\right\}\right|\leq b_{j}K

which indicates

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},\mathcal{G}_{j,p}^{\mu}\right\}\leq\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu})\right\}.

(122)

Given an item
i\in E\setminus S^{\star}
, assume it is included
v_{i}
solutions in
\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
, we index them according to the decreasing order of their mean gaps, i.e.
\{A^{i,k}\}_{k\in[v_{i}]}
with
\Delta_{A^{i,1}}\geq\ldots\geq\Delta_{A^{i,v_{i}}}
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{E}\right\}

(123)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}

(124)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},\mathcal{G}_{j,p}^{\mu}\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}

(125)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{i\in A_{p}\setminus S^{\star}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu})\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}

(126)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{i\in E\setminus S^{\star}}\sum_{k\in[v_{i}]}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A^{i,k}=A_{p},i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}(\Delta_{A^{i,k}},\omega_{\mu})\right\}\Delta_{A^{i,k}}

(127)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in E\setminus S^{\star}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{k\in[v_{i}]}\frac{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}}{b_{j}K}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A^{i,k}=A_{p},i\in A^{i,k},T_{i}(p-1)\leq\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}^{2}}+D\right)\right\}

(128)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\sum_{i\in E\setminus S^{\star}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{k\in[v_{i}]}\frac{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}}{b_{j}K}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A^{i,k}=A_{p},i\in A^{i,k},T_{i}(p-1)\leq\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)\right\}

(129)

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\sum_{i\in E\setminus S^{\star}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)\left(\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,v_{i}}}}+\sum_{k=2}^{v_{i}-1}\Delta_{A^{i,k+1}}\left(\frac{1}{\Delta^{2}_{A^{i,k+1}}}-\frac{1}{\Delta^{2}_{A^{i,k}}}\right)\right)

(130)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in E\setminus S^{\star}}\frac{2C\gamma K}{\Delta_{A^{i,v_{i}}}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)

(131)

where
(a)
and
b
make use of Lemma
B.1
and Lemma
B.2
,
(c)
is obtained by relaxing
\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}}
to
\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,v_{i}}}}
,
(d)
is obtained by solving the optimization problem.

Case
2
:
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}

For the case where
\omega_{\mu}\leq\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}
, denote

\bar{\omega}:=\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}}{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}}}
.
Given an item
i\in E
, assume it is included
v_{i}
solutions in
\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}
, we index them according to the decreasing order of their gaps

\bar{\Delta}_{A}:=\max\left\{\bar{\omega}\Delta_{A},\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A}}{3}\right\}

, i.e.
\{A^{i,k}\}_{k\in[v_{i}]}
with
\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,1}}\geq\ldots\geq\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,v_{i}}}
.
Denote
c_{i}:=\max_{k\in[v_{i}]}\left(\frac{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}}{\bar{\Delta}_{{A^{i,k}}}}\right)^{2}
,

k_{i}=\argmax_{k\in[v_{i}]}\Delta_{A^{i,k}}
and

d_{i}=\min_{k\in[v_{i}]}\Delta_{A^{i,k}}
, i.e., the minimum mean gap.

We have

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{E}\right\}

(132)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B},\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu},\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}

(133)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B},\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\max\left\{\bar{\omega}\Delta_{A_{p}},\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}

(134)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B},\mathcal{G}_{j,p}\left(\max\left\{\bar{\omega}\Delta_{\mu},\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}

(135)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\bar{\Delta}_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}\Delta_{A_{p}}

(136)

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{i\in E\setminus S^{\star}}\sum_{k\in[v_{i}]}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A^{i,k}=A_{p},i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\bar{\Delta}_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}\Delta_{A^{i,k}}

(137)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in E}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{k\in[v_{i}]}\frac{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}}{b_{j}K}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A^{i,k}=A_{p},i\in A^{i,k},T_{i}(p-1)\leq\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,k}}^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,k}}^{2}}+D\right)\right\}

(138)

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\sum_{i\in E}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{k\in[v_{i}]}\frac{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}}{b_{j}K}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A^{i,k}=A_{p},i\in A^{i,k},T_{i}(p-1)\leq\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,k}}^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)\right\}

(139)

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}\sum_{i\in E}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)\left(\frac{c_{i}}{d_{i}}+c_{i}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{d_{i}}-\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,k_{i}}}}\right)\right)

(140)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in E}\frac{2c_{i}\cdot C\gamma K}{d_{i}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)

(141)

where
(a)
comes from Lemma
B.1
,
(b)
results from Lemma
B.3
,
(c)
is due to Lemma
B.2
,
(d)
is obtained by relaxing
\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,k}}^{2}}
to
\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}
and
(e)
is achieved by solving the optimization problem in (
139
):

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T}\sum_{k\in[v_{i}]}\frac{\Delta_{A^{i,k}}}{b_{j}K}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A^{i,k}=A_{p},i\in A^{i,k},T_{i}(p-1)\leq\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,k}}^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)\right\}

(142)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)\cdot\left(\frac{c_{i}}{d_{i}}+\int_{d_{i}}^{\Delta_{A^{i,k_{i}}}}\frac{c_{i}}{x^{2}}\,\mathrm{dx}\right)

(143)

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\bar{\Delta}_{A^{i,v_{i}}}^{2}}+D\right)\left(\frac{c_{i}}{d_{i}}+c_{i}\cdot\left(\frac{1}{d_{i}}-\frac{1}{\Delta_{A^{i,k_{i}}}}\right)\right)

(144)

In conclusion
,
for
i\in E\setminus S^{\star}
, denote

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},\min}:=\min_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}\Delta_{S}

(145)

For
i\in E
, denote

\displaystyle\Delta_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B},\min}

\displaystyle:=\min_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}\Delta_{S}

(146)

\displaystyle\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle:=\min_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}\max\{\bar{\omega}\Delta_{S},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}/3\}

(147)

\displaystyle c_{i}

\displaystyle:=\max_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}\left(\frac{\Delta_{S}}{\max\{\bar{\omega}\Delta_{S},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}/3\}}\right)^{2}

(148)

The regret due to suboptimality can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{1}(T)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in E\setminus S^{\star}}\frac{2C\gamma K}{\Delta_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},\min}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\Delta_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},\min}^{2}}+D\right)

(149)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\in E}\frac{2c_{i}\cdot C\gamma K}{\Delta_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B},\min}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{(\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{\prime})^{2}}+D\right)

(150)

∎

B.4

Regret due to safeness-checking

We firstly introduce two more technical lemmas in order to upper bound the regret.
We characterize the number of sub-solutions
n_{p}
in Algorithm
2
by the following lemma.

Lemma B.4
.

At any phase
p
, we have
\mathbb{P}[n_{p}\leq Q]=1
. Furthermore,
if
U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)\in((m-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2},m\bar{\sigma}^{2}]
for some
m\in\mathbb{N}
, then
m\leq n_{p}\leq 2m-1.

Proof of Lemma
B.4
.

Recall that an absolutely safe solution
S
is safe w.p.
1
and
S\in\mathcal{S}_{p-1}
, thus
|A_{p,r}|\geq q,\forall r\in[n_{p}-1]
.
If

n_{p}>Q
, i.e.
n_{p}\geq Q+1
, then

\displaystyle K\geq|A_{p}|=\sum_{r=1}^{n_{p}}|A_{p,r}|>\sum_{r=1}^{Q}|A_{p,r}|\geq Q\cdot q\geq K

(151)

which constitutes a contradiction. Therefore, we have
\mathbb{P}[n_{p}\leq Q]=1
.

If
U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)\in((m-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2},m\bar{\sigma}^{2}]
for some
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, we sequentially define
\{j_{1},j_{2},\ldots\}\subset[|A_{p}|]
as follows: denote
j_{0}:=0
and let
j_{1}
be the integer such that

\sum_{s=1}^{j_{1}-1}U_{i_{s}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)\leq\bar{\sigma}^{2}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\sum_{s=1}^{j_{1}}U_{i_{s}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>\bar{\sigma}^{2}.

Then let
j_{2}>j_{1}
be the integer such that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=j_{1}+1}^{j_{2}-1}U_{i_{s}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)\leq\bar{\sigma}^{2}\quad

\displaystyle\mbox{and}\quad\sum_{s=j_{1}+1}^{j_{2}}U_{i_{s}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>\bar{\sigma}^{2}.

(152)

The last integer
j_{k}=|A_{p}|
satisfies

0<\sum_{s=j_{k-1}+1}^{j_{k}}U_{i_{s}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)\leq\bar{\sigma}^{2}.

If
k\geq m+1
, we must have

\displaystyle U_{A_{t}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{k}\sum_{s=j_{l-1}+1}^{j_{l}}U_{i_{s}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)

(154)

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{l=1}^{m}\sum_{s=j_{l-1}+1}^{j_{l}}U_{i_{s}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>m\bar{\sigma}^{2}

(155)

which contradicts with
U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)\in((m-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2},m\bar{\sigma}^{2}]
. Hence,
k\leq m
.
Then we construct the sub-solutions by

\displaystyle A_{p,l}=\{i_{j_{l-1}+1},\ldots,i_{j_{l}-1}\},\quad\forall l\in[k-1]

(156)

\displaystyle\mbox{and}\quad A_{p,k}=\{i_{j_{k-1}+1},\ldots,i_{j_{k}}\}.

(157)

There are
k-1
items
\{i_{j_{l}}:l\in[k-1]\}
left which will compose at most
k-1
additional sub-solutions.
In conclusion, we need at most
2m-1
sub-solutions, i.e.,
n_{p}\leq 2m-1
. Obviously, we need at least
m
sub-solutions since
\bar{\sigma}^{2}>\sigma^{2}
. So
m\leq n_{p}\leq 2m-1
.
∎

Remark B.5
.

Indexing the items in Line
3
of
Greedy-Split
can be done arbitrarily, i.e., it does not require any specific order of the items.
As such,
Greedy-Split
is an efficient greedy algorithm. We note that finding the optimal order that leads to the minimum number of sub-solutions
n_{p}
is a combinatorial problem which is generally hard to solve.

Due to the fact that the upper confidence of any solution
S
satisfies
U_{S}^{\mathrm{v}}(p)\leq Q\bar{\sigma}^{2}
, thus
n_{p}
can at most be
2Q-1
.

Lemma
6.1
implies the key to upper bound the regret due to safeness-checking is to upper bound
\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}
over the horizon
T
.
From the definition of
\mathcal{U}_{p}(r):=\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\}
, for
r_{1},r_{2}\in[Q-1]
with
r_{1}>r_{2}
, event
\mathcal{U}_{p}(r_{1})
indicates event
\mathcal{U}_{p}(r_{2})
. Thus in order to upper bound
\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}
, it suffices to upper bound
\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}
for
r\in[Q-1]
. To be more specific, given
l\in[Q-1]
, in order to compute the maximum number of times
\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}\geq l
, we only need to compute the maximum number of times event
\mathcal{U}_{p}(l)
occurs.

In the following lemma, we show a necessary condition (in terms of event
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)
) for event
\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)
.

Lemma B.6
.

On the event

\mathcal{E}
,

•

for
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}
:

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(158)

•

for
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}-\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(159)

Proof of Lemma
B.6
.

The proof is straightforward

\displaystyle U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}

(160)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\Rightarrow}\quad

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{A_{p}}^{2}(p-1)+\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\beta_{\mathrm{u}}(T_{i}(p-1))>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}

(161)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\Rightarrow}\quad

\displaystyle\sigma_{A_{p}}^{2}+2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\beta_{\mathrm{u}}(T_{i}(p-1))>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}

(162)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\quad

\displaystyle 2\sum_{i\in A_{p}}3\cdot\mathrm{lil}(T_{i}(p-1),\omega_{\mathrm{v}})>(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+(\bar{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma_{A_{p}}^{2})

(163)

where
(a)
is due to the definition of the confidence bounds for a solution (
13
), and
(b)
utilizes the event
\bigcap_{i\in A_{p}}\mathcal{E}_{i,T_{i}(p-1)}
.
For
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}
, the above event is equivalent to

\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)
; and for
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}
, it is equivalent to

\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}-\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)
.
∎

The above lemma and Lemma
B.1
upper bound the components in
\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})
by the
\mathcal{F}
events.

We are now ready to bound the regret due to safeness checking.

See
6.3

Proof of Lemma
6.3
.

From Lemma
6.1
, the high-probability regret due to safeness-checking is

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\mu^{\star}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\left[2\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}\right]

(164)

\displaystyle=2\mu^{\star}\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}

(165)

In the following, given
r\in[Q-1]
, we are going to upper bound
\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}
conditional on event
\mathcal{E}
.
When
U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}
holds, there are at most
2r+1
solutions being chosen at phase
p
, i.e.
n_{p}\leq 2r+1
, according to Lemma
B.4
.
Therefore, we are also deriving an upper bound for number of phases in which there are at most
2r+1
sub-solutions being sampled.

The proof scheme is planned as follows:
in
Step 1
,
we decompose the event
\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)
into
4
events according to where
A_{p}
lies, i.e. (1)
A_{p}=S^{\star}
; (2)
\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
; (3)
\mathcal{R}
and (4)
\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}
. We will upper bound the regret under each of these cases.

In
Step 2
, we apply Lemma
B.1
to upper bound the number of times a solution
A
can be selected via the events
\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}
and
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)
. Because there will be multiple
\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu}
and
\mathcal{F}_{p}(x,\omega)
events in the indicator function, Lemma
B.3
will be adopted to merge them into one event.
After that, Lemma
B.2
is utilized to bridge the number of times a solution is identified to the number of times of an item is sampled. At the end of this step, we conclude the number of times
\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}
occurs under the four cases.

In
Step 3
, we upper bound
\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}

based on the results from Step 2.

Step 1:

We decompose
\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}
conditional on event
\mathcal{E}
into four parts:

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\}

(166)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}

(167)

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\Big(\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}=S^{\star}\right\}+\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}+\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\right\}+\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\Big)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}

(168)

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}=S^{\star}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}+\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}

(169)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}+\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\Big)\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}

(170)

Step 2:
For each of the scenarios, we firstly upper bound the regret by the “
\mathcal{F}
events” and they can be further bounded in terms of “
\mathcal{G}
events”.

Case 1:
A_{p}=S^{\star}

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}=S^{\star}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}

(172)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}=S^{\star}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(173)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}=S^{\star}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{G}_{j,p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(174)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}=S^{\star}\right\}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(175)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\mathbbm{1}\left\{T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(176)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}m_{j}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)

(177)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\frac{9}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})^{2}}+D\right)

(178)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}C\cdot\frac{9\cdot\gamma K}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}}}^{2}}+D\right)

(179)

where
(a)
utilizes Lemma
B.6
,
(b)
makes use of Lemma
B.2
and
(c)
follows the definition of
\mathcal{G}_{j,p}
.
For simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle g_{S^{\star}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})=C\cdot\frac{9\cdot\gamma K}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}}}^{2}}+D\right).

(180)

Thus

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}=S^{\star}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}\leq\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}g_{S^{\star}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})

(181)

Case 2:
A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}

Under this case, there will be a comparison between
\omega_{\mu}
and
\omega_{\mathrm{v}}
thus we denote

\tilde{\omega}=\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}}{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}}
.
For
i\in E
, denote
\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}:=\min_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{S}}{\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}},\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}\right\}
which is achieved by solution
S_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}
,
and assume
\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}\in(\frac{r_{i}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}},\frac{(r_{i}+1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}]
for some
r_{i}\in\mathbb{N}
.

Scenario 1:
\omega_{\mu}\geq\omega_{\mathrm{v}}

We firstly deal with the case where
\omega_{\mu}\geq\omega_{\mathrm{v}}
, i.e.,
\tilde{\omega}\leq 1
.
We have

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}

(182)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu},\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(183)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu}\right),\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(184)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\max\left\{\Delta_{A_{p}},\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mu}\right)\right\}

(185)

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{G}_{j,p}\left(\max\left\{\Delta_{A_{p}},\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mu}\right)\right\}

(186)

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\Delta_{A_{p}},\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mu}\right)\right\}

(187)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in E}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\Delta_{A_{p}},\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mu}\right)\right\}

(188)

where
(a)
utilizes Lemma
B.6
,

(b)
and
(c)
make use of Lemma
B.3
,

(d)
is due to Lemma
B.2

and
(e)
follows the definition of
\mathcal{G}_{j,p}
.

Given
i\in E
,

(1) if
r\geq r_{i}+2
, for any
S\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
that contains item
i
,

\displaystyle\max\left\{\Delta_{S},\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3}\right\}\geq\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3}\geq\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r_{i}+1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3}\geq\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}.

(189)

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\Delta_{A_{p}},\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mu}\right)\right\}

(190)

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3},\omega_{\mu}\right)\right\}

(191)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}m_{j}\left(\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3},\omega_{\mu}\right)

(192)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\frac{9}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\omega}^{2}}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{9}{(\tilde{\omega}(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\omega}^{2}}D\right)

(193)

\displaystyle\leq C\cdot\frac{9\cdot\gamma K}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\omega}^{2}}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\omega}^{2}}D\right)

(194)

\displaystyle=C\cdot\frac{\gamma K}{(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}D\right)

(195)

(2) if
r\leq r_{i}+1
, for any
S\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
that contains item
i

\displaystyle\max\left\{\Delta_{S},\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3}\right\}\geq\max\left\{\Delta_{S},\tilde{\omega}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3}\right\}\geq\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}.

(196)

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\Delta_{A_{p}},\tilde{\omega}\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mu}\right)\right\}

(197)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}},\omega_{\mu}\right)\right\}

(198)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}m_{j}\left(\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}},\omega_{\mu}\right)

(199)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\frac{1}{(\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{(\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mu}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}+D\right)

(200)

\displaystyle\leq C\cdot\frac{\gamma K}{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}D\right)

(201)

where
(a)
is achieved by sampling
A_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}
.
Therefore, if we denote
g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},1}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}):=

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{C\gamma K}{(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}D\right),r\geq\left\lfloor\frac{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor+2\\
&\frac{C\gamma K}{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}D\right),r\leq\left\lfloor\frac{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor+1\end{aligned}\right.

(202)

then (
188
) can be upper bounded by

\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},1}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})

Scenario 2:
\omega_{\mu}\leq\omega_{\mathrm{v}}

For the case where
\omega_{\mu}\leq\omega_{\mathrm{v}}
, i.e.,
\tilde{\omega}\geq 1
.
We have

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}

(203)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}^{\mu},\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(204)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\Delta_{A_{p}},\omega_{\mu}\right),\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(205)

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{F}_{p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{A_{p}}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(206)

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{G}_{j,p}\left(\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{A_{p}}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(207)

\displaystyle\overset{(e)}{\leq}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{i\in A_{p}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{A_{p}}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(208)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in E}\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{A_{p}}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(209)

Given
i\in E
,

(1) if
r\geq r_{i}+2
, for any
S\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
that contains item
i
,

\displaystyle\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{S}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3}\right\}\geq\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3}\geq\frac{(r_{i}+1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3}\geq\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}.

(210)

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{A_{p}}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(211)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(212)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}m_{j}\left(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)

(213)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\frac{9}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{9}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2})^{2}}+D\right)

(214)

\displaystyle\leq C\cdot\frac{9\cdot\gamma K}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+D\right)

(215)

\displaystyle=C\cdot\frac{\gamma K}{(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}D\right)

(216)

(2) if
r\leq r_{i}+1
, for any
S\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
that contains item
i
,

\displaystyle\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{S}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3}\right\}\geq\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{S}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3}\right\}\geq\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}.

(217)

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{A_{p}}}{\tilde{\omega}},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(218)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(219)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}m_{j}\left(\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)

(220)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\frac{1}{(\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{(\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}+D\right)

(221)

\displaystyle\leq C\cdot\frac{\gamma K}{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}D\right)

(222)

Therefore, if we denote
g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},2}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}):=

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{C\gamma K}{(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}D\right),r\geq\left\lfloor\frac{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor+2\\

then (
209
) can be upper bounded by

\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B},2}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}).

In conclusion, for
i\in E
, we denote
\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}:=\min_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{S}}{\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}},\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}\right\}
,

and
g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}):=

&\frac{C\gamma K}{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+D\right)\right),r\leq\left\lfloor\frac{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor+1\end{aligned}\right.

(224)

then

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}\leq\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})

(225)

Case 3:
A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}

Under this case, there will be a comparison between
\omega_{\mathrm{v}}
and
\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}
thus we denote

\bar{\omega}=\sqrt{\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}}{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}}

i\in E

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\bar{\omega}\cdot\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}-\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\right)\right\}

i
,

\displaystyle\max\left\{\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}-\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3}\right\}\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A}}{3},\frac{\bar{\omega}}{1+\bar{\omega}}\cdot\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3}\right\}\geq\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3}

(260)

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\max\left\{\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3},\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}-\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{A_{p}}}{3}\right\},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(261)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{R}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{i\in A_{p},T_{i}(p-1)\leq m_{j}\left(\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)\right\}

(262)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{1}{b_{j}K}m_{j}\left(\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\right)

(263)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j\in\mathbb{N}}\frac{a_{j}\cdot\gamma K^{2}}{b_{j}K}\frac{1}{(\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{(\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3})^{2}}+D\right)

(264)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\gamma K}{(\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{(\frac{1}{\bar{\omega}}\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3})^{2}}+D\right)

(265)

\displaystyle=\frac{C\gamma K}{(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}+D\right)\right)

(266)

Therefore, if we denote

\displaystyle g_{\mathcal{R},2}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}):=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{C\gamma K}{(\frac{(r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),r\geq\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor+2\\

(267)

then (
252
) can be upper bounded by

i\in E

then

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{p}\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}\right\}\mathbbm{1}\left\{U_{A_{p}}^{\mathrm{v}}(p-1)>r\bar{\sigma}^{2}\right\}\leq\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})

(302)

Step 3:

According to the results in Step 2, given
r\in[Q-1]
, the event
\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)
can happen at most

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}

(303)

\displaystyle\leq\min\left\{T^{\prime},\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}g_{S^{\star}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{R}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})\right\}

(304)

phases, where the
g
functions are defined in (
292
), (
294
), (
297
) and (
300
). By Lemma
B.4
,
(1) event
\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)
indicates
r+1\leq n_{p}
, thus (
303
) also indicates at most in this number of phases there are at least
r+1
being pulled at each phase.
(2) event
\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\cap\mathcal{U}_{p}(r+1)^{c}
indicates
r+1\leq n_{p}\leq 2r+1
, i.e., there are at least
r+1
and at most
2r+1
solutions being pulled at each phase.
For
r\in[Q]
, we denote

\displaystyle T^{\prime}_{r}:=\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}g_{S^{\star}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{R}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}),\quad r\in[Q-1]

(305)

and
T^{\prime}_{Q}:=0,T^{\prime}_{0}=\infty
.
Note that the
g
functions are increasing as
r
decreases, so if there exists an
r^{\prime}\in[Q]
, such that
T^{\prime}\in[T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}},T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}-1})
(or
T^{\prime}\leq T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}-1}
) , then

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}

(306)

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=1}^{r^{\prime}-1}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}+\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}

(307)

\displaystyle\leq T^{\prime}\cdot(r^{\prime}-1)+\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}g_{S^{\star}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{R}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}})+\sum_{i\in E}g_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})

(308)

\displaystyle=T^{\prime}\cdot(r^{\prime}-1)+\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}T^{\prime}_{r}

(309)

\displaystyle\leq T^{\prime}\cdot(r^{\prime}-1)+\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}h_{S^{\star}}(r^{\prime},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r^{\prime},\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{R}}(r^{\prime},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r^{\prime},\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})

(310)

\displaystyle=T^{\prime}\cdot(r^{\prime}-1)+H(r^{\prime},\Lambda)

(311)

where

\displaystyle H(r^{\prime},\Lambda):=\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}h_{S^{\star}}(r^{\prime},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r^{\prime},\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{R}}(r^{\prime},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r^{\prime},\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})

(312)

and the
h
functions are defined at the end of the proof.
This indicates, when
T^{\prime}\leq T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}-1}
, the upper bound of the high-probability regret due to safeness-checking
\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})
(hence the the total regret) is lower bounded by a linear function with slope
r^{\prime}-1
.
In particular, the upper bound of
\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})
is lower bounded by a linear function when
T^{\prime}\leq T^{\prime}_{1}
and it remains a constant when
T^{\prime}>T^{\prime}_{1}
.
We can compute an upper bound for the number of solutions being pulled during these
T^{\prime}
phases when
T^{\prime}\in[T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}},T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}-1})
:

\displaystyle T^{\prime}_{Q-1}\cdot(2Q-1)+(T^{\prime}_{Q-2}-T^{\prime}_{Q-1})\cdot(2Q-3)+\cdots+(T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}}-T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}+1})\cdot(2r^{\prime}+1)+(T^{\prime}-T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}})(2r^{\prime}-1)

(313)

\displaystyle=T^{\prime}+2\left(T^{\prime}_{Q-1}\cdot(Q-1)+(T^{\prime}_{Q-2}-T^{\prime}_{Q-1})\cdot(Q-2)+\cdots+(T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}}-T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}+1})\cdot r^{\prime}+(T^{\prime}-T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}})(r^{\prime}-1)\right)

(314)

\displaystyle=(2r^{\prime}-1)T^{\prime}+2\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}T^{\prime}_{r}

(315)

\displaystyle\leq(2r^{\prime}-1)T^{\prime}+2H(r^{\prime},\Lambda)

(316)

Thus, the number of pulled solutions is at most
2H(1,\Lambda)
.
1
1

1

Note that the upper bound for the regret is
2\mu^{\star}T^{\prime}\cdot(r^{\prime}-1)+2\mu^{\star}\cdot H(r^{\prime},\Lambda)
and the upper bound for the number of solutions is
(2r^{\prime}-1)T^{\prime}+2H(r^{\prime},\Lambda)
, which indicates we can roughly use
\min\{T\mu^{\star},2\mu^{\star}H(r^{\prime},\Lambda)\}
to bound the regret due to safeness-checking with
T
time steps.

In conclusion
, the regret due to safeness-checking can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\mathrm{R}_{2}(T^{\prime})

\displaystyle=2\mu^{\star}\sum_{r=1}^{Q-1}\sum_{p=1}^{T^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathcal{U}_{p}(r)\right\}

(317)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu^{\star}T^{\prime}\cdot(r^{\prime}-1)

(318)

\displaystyle\quad+2\mu^{\star}\left(\sum_{i\in S^{\star}}h_{S^{\star}}(r^{\prime},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r^{\prime},\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{R}}(r^{\prime},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}})+\sum_{i\in E}h_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r^{\prime},\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})\right)

(319)

\displaystyle=2\mu^{\star}T^{\prime}\cdot(r^{\prime}-1)+2\mu^{\star}\cdot H(r^{\prime},\Lambda)

(320)

where
T^{\prime}\in[T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}},T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}-1})
with
T^{\prime}_{r^{\prime}}
defined in (
305
),
for
i\in E
,

\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}:=\min_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{S}}{\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}},\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}\right\}
,
\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}:=\min_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{R}}\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}
and

\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}:=\min_{S\ni i,S\in\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{S}}{\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu})}},\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}}\right\}.

The
h
functions:

(For convenience, we restate the notations:

\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}}:=\max\{\omega_{\mu},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}\}
,
\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}}:=\max\{\omega_{\mathrm{v}},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\}
,
\omega_{\max}:=\max\{\omega_{\mu},\omega_{\mathrm{v}},\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}\}
and
\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}:=\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}}+\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}}

For
S^{\star}
: for each
i\in S^{\star}

\displaystyle h_{S^{\star}}(r^{\prime},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}}):=\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}g_{S^{\star}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})

(321)

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}\frac{9\cdot C\gamma K}{((r-1)\bar{\sigma}^{2}+\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}}}^{2}}+D\right)

(322)

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{18\cdot C\gamma K}{(r^{\prime}-1)\bar{\sigma}^{4}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{(\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})^{2}}+D\right),\quad r^{\prime}\geq 2\\
&\frac{18\cdot C\gamma K}{(\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})^{2}}\left(2\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}}+\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{(\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{S^{\star}})^{2}}+D\right),\quad r^{\prime}=1\end{aligned}\right.

(323)

For
\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}
: for each
i\in E
, there is a changing point
\left\lfloor\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}}\right\rfloor+2
in
g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})
, thus

\displaystyle h_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r^{\prime},\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}):=\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}g_{\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}})

(324)

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &0,r^{\prime}=Q\\
&(Q-r^{\prime})\frac{C\gamma K}{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}}\right\rfloor\geq Q-3\\
&\frac{2\cdot C\gamma K}{(r^{\prime}-1)(\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}}\right\rfloor+2\leq r^{\prime}<Q-1\\
&\frac{3\cdot C\gamma K}{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}}\right\rfloor=0,r^{\prime}=1\\
&C\gamma K\left(\frac{4}{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}})}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}}-\frac{r^{\prime}-1}{\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}\right)\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mu\mathrm{v}})\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}\cap\mathcal{B}}^{2}}+D\right)\right),\text{otherwise}\end{aligned}\right.

(325)

For
\mathcal{R}
: for each
i\in E
, there is a changing point
\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor+2
, thus

\displaystyle h_{\mathcal{R}}(r^{\prime},\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}):=\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}g_{\mathcal{R}}(r,\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}})\leq

(326)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &0,r^{\prime}=Q\\
&(Q-r^{\prime})\frac{C\gamma K}{(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}})(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor\geq Q-3\\
&\frac{2C\gamma K}{(r^{\prime}-1)(\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}})(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime}}}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor+2\leq r^{\prime}<Q-1\\
&\frac{3C\gamma K}{(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}})(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}\bar{\sigma}^{2}}\right\rfloor=0,r^{\prime}=1\\
&C\gamma K\left(\frac{3}{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}}\cdot\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}}}-\frac{r^{\prime}-1}{(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}\right)\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}\mathrm{v}^{\prime}})(\frac{\Delta^{\mathrm{v}}_{i,\mathcal{R}}}{3\sqrt{\ln(1/\omega_{\mathrm{v}}^{\prime})}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),\text{else}\end{aligned}\right.

(327)

For
\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}
: for each
i\in E
, there is a changing point
\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}/3}\right\rfloor+2
, thus

\displaystyle h_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r^{\prime},\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}):=\sum_{r=r^{\prime}}^{Q-1}g_{\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}(r,\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})

(328)

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &0,r^{\prime}=Q\\
&(Q-r^{\prime})\frac{C\gamma K}{(\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\max})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\max})(\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}/3}\right\rfloor\geq Q-3\\
&\frac{2C\gamma K}{(r^{\prime}-1)(\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\max})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\max})(\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}/3}\right\rfloor+2\leq r^{\prime}<Q-1\\
&\frac{3C\gamma K}{(\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\max})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\max})(\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),\left\lfloor\frac{\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}\cdot\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}}{\bar{\sigma}^{2}/3}\right\rfloor=0,r^{\prime}=1\\
&C\gamma K\left(\frac{3}{\frac{\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{3\omega_{\mathrm{sum}}}\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}}}-\frac{r^{\prime}-1}{(\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}\right)\left(2+\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\max})}\left(\ln\ln_{+}\frac{1}{\ln(1/\omega_{\max})(\bar{\Delta}_{i,\mathcal{S}^{c}\cap\mathcal{B}})^{2}}+D\right)\right),\text{otherwise}\end{aligned}\right.

(329)
